Like I said a minute ago... no. I don't really remember it being an issue there. My parents mostly just let me be.\n\nDoctor: Before age 12, did you often have trouble paying close attention to details or make careless mistakes in schoolwork?\n\nPatient: I don't... really remember that. So... that's why I'm here.\n\nDoctor: Did you have similar trouble paying attention or getting distracted when you were a child?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess. Um... I don't really think about it much, but... looking back, it was kind of an issue when I was a kid, too. \n\nTeachers were always moving my seat around. Just... saying I talked too much, or I'd get up without asking. Sometimes I'd, you know, blurt things out before they even finished a question. So... yeah. I suppose so.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling nervous or worried a lot of the time?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean... I'm busy, obviously, running a company, but... no. I wouldn't say I'm nervous or worried. Just... busy.\n\nDoctor: Do these attention and impulsivity problems cause trouble in more than one part of your life, such as at work and at home?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I suppose so. I mean, I’m still keeping up overall. But... well, it’s starting to cost me a bit of trust at home, I guess. When family plans don't go the way they should. \n\nAnd at work... some people are losing patience with me. Just... in meetings, or mentoring conversations, when things get derailed. So... yeah. Both places.\n\nDoctor: Do these problems with attention and impulsivity still happen now, most days?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I mean, it's pretty constant. But it's really been... well, it's been harder to keep a lid on it for the last year or so. Maybe a year and a couple of months. Ever since... well, there's been some stuff with my in-laws. It's just taking up a lot of space at home, and... yeah. Since then, it's been worse.\n\nDoctor: Before this past year, was there any stretch of at least six months when you did not have these attention problems?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I've never really kept track of it, but... it's always just been how I am, I guess. I can't really think of a time when it just stopped.\n\nDoctor: Do you often lose things you need for tasks, like keys, paperwork, or your phone?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean... I usually know where my stuff is. I have an assistant who handles a lot of the paperwork anyway, so... it's not really an issue.\n\nDoctor: Before age 12, did you have similar problems with inattention or being easily distracted?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I suppose. Looking back... it was like that when I was a kid, too. Teachers were always moving my seat around. Said I talked too much, or... I'd just get up without asking. Blurt things out before they even finished a question. So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Do you also often feel unusually restless or like you have to move around a lot?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I suppose. If I'm supposed to just sit still... like at family dinners, or those church volunteer meetings. It just... feels like there's a motor running in me. I usually need to move, or just do something with my hands. But... yeah.\n\nDoctor: Before you were 12 years old, did these attention or activity symptoms cause problems at home or school?\n\nPatient: I don't really know. I mean... I guess the teachers got annoyed, but... I wouldn't really say it was a big problem. It was just... how things were. I don't really remember it being a major issue at home or anything.\n\nDoctor: When you were a child, did these attention or activity symptoms also happen at home?\n\nPatient: Like I said... I don't really remember it being a thing there. If it was, my parents didn't make a big deal out of it. I just... did my own thing at home, I guess. So... no, not really.\n\nDoctor: Before age 12, did these attention or activity symptoms cause you to have trouble in school?\n\nPatient: I mean... yeah, I suppose. Looking back... teachers were always moving my seat around back then. Said I talked too much... or I'd get up without asking. Sometimes I'd just... blurt things out before they even finished a question. So... I guess you could call that trouble.\n\nDoctor: Before age 12, do you remember these attention or activity symptoms causing you problems at home?\n\nPatient: I mean... didn't we just cover that? Like I said a minute ago... no. I don't really remember it being an issue there. My parents mostly just let me be.\n\nDoctor: Before age 12, did you often have trouble paying close attention to details or make careless mistakes in schoolwork?\n\nPatient: I don't... really remember that. I mean, I don't think I had a problem with details or whatever. I just... didn't really notice that kind of thing, I guess. So... no.\n\nDoctor: When you were a child, did these attention or activity symptoms also happen at home?\n\nPatient: I thought we already talked about this. I literally just told you.
